Its data format is widely used for power system analysis. This skill documents the MATPOWER case format for parsing power system data.\n\n## Overview\n\nA MATPOWER case contains the following key arrays:\n- `baseMVA`: Base power in MVA (typically 100)\n- `bus`: Bus data matrix\n- `gen`: Generator data matrix\n- `branch`: Branch (transmission line/transformer) data matrix\n- `gencost`: Generator cost data matrix\n\n## Bus Data (bus array)\n\nEach row represents a bus with columns:\n\n| Index | Name | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| 0 | BUS_I | Bus number (1-indexed) |\n| 1 | BUS_TYPE | 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack/Reference |\n| 2 | PD | Real power demand (MW) |\n| 3 | QD | Reactive power demand (MVAr) |\n| 4 | GS | Shunt conductance (MW at V=1.0 p.u.) |\n| 5 | BS | Shunt susceptance (MVAr at V=1.0 p.u.) |\n| 6 | BUS_AREA | Area number |\n| 7 | VM | Voltage magnitude (p.u.) |\n| 8 | VA | Voltage angle (degrees) |\n| 9 | BASE_KV | Base voltage (kV) |\n| 10 | ZONE | Loss zone |\n| 11 | VMAX | Maximum voltage magnitude (p.u.) |\n| 12 | VMIN | Minimum voltage magnitude (p.u.) |\n\n## Generator Data (gen array)\n\nEach row represents a generator with columns:\n\n| Index | Name | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| 0 | GEN_BUS | Bus number (1-indexed) |\n| 1 | PG | Real power output (MW) |\n| 2 | QG | Reactive power output (MVAr) |\n| 3 | QMAX | Maximum reactive power (MVAr) |\n| 4 | QMIN | Minimum reactive power (MVAr) |\n| 5 | VG | Voltage setpoint (p.u.) |\n| 6 | MBASE | Machine MVA base |\n| 7 | GEN_STATUS | 1=in-service, 0=out-of-service |\n| 8 | PMAX | Maximum real power output (MW) |\n| 9 | PMIN | Minimum real power output (MW) |\n\n## Branch Data (branch array)\n\nEach row represents a transmission line or transformer with columns:\n\n| Index | Name | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| 0 | F_BUS | From bus number (1-indexed) |\n| 1 | T_BUS | To bus number (1-indexed) |\n| 2 | BR_R | Resistance (p.u.) |\n| 3 | BR_X | Reactance (p.u.) |\n| 4 | BR_B | Total line charging susceptance (p.u.) |\n| 5 | RATE_A | MVA rating A (long term rating) |\n| 6 | RATE_B | MVA rating B (short term rating) |\n| 7 | RATE_C | MVA rating C (emergency rating) |\n| 8 | TAP | Transformer tap ratio (0 = transmission line) |\n| 9 | SHIFT | Transformer phase shift angle (degrees) |\n| 10 | BR_STATUS | 1=in-service, 0=out-of-service |\n| 11 | ANGMIN | Minimum angle difference (degrees) |\n| 12 | ANGMAX | Maximum angle difference (degrees) |\n\n## Generator Cost Data (gencost array)\n\nFor polynomial cost model (MODEL=2):\n\n| Index | Name | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| 0 | MODEL | Cost model type (2=polynomial) |\n| 1 | STARTUP | Startup cost ($) |\n| 2 | SHUTDOWN | Shutdown cost ($) |\n| 3 | NCOST | Number of cost coefficients |\n| 4+ | COST | Coefficients (highest order first) |\n\nExample: For a quadratic cost `C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c`:\n- NCOST = 3\n- COST = [a, b, c]\n\n## Python Code Example\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef load_matpower_json(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER case from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    bus = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus,\n        'gen': gen,\n        'branch': branch,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\n# Access bus IDs (convert to int for indexing)\nbus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n# Access loads at each bus\nPd = bus[:, 2]  # Real power demand in MW\nQd = bus[:, 3]  # Reactive power demand in MVAr\n\n# Access generator data\ngen_bus = gen[:, 0].astype(int)  # Bus where generator is connected\nPmax = gen[:, 8]  # Maximum power output\nPmin = gen[:, 9]  # Minimum power output\n\n# Access branch data\nfrom_bus = branch[:, 0].astype(int)\nto_bus = branch[:, 1].astype(int)\nreactance = branch[:, 3]\nrate_a = branch[:, 5]  # Thermal limit in MVA\n```\n\n## Per-Unit Conversion\n\nPower quantities in the data are in MW/MVAr. For DC power flow calculations:\n- Convert to per-unit: P_pu = P_MW / baseMVA\n- Reactance is already in per-unit on the system base\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2727,"cache_read_input_tokens":25484,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2727,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fb895624-8769-4efe-ba21-a976446976e0","uuid":"e0641237-6aca-4bab-be5e-fed05d16d246"}

Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| 0 | MODEL | Cost model type (2=polynomial) |\n| 1 | STARTUP | Startup cost ($) |\n| 2 | SHUTDOWN | Shutdown cost ($) |\n| 3 | NCOST | Number of cost coefficients |\n| 4+ | COST | Coefficients (highest order first) |\n\nExample: For a quadratic cost `C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c`:\n- NCOST = 3\n- COST = [a, b, c]\n\n## Python Code Example\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef load_matpower_json(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER case from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n    bus = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus,\n        'gen': gen,\n        'branch': branch,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\n# Access bus IDs (convert to int for indexing)\nbus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n\n# Access loads at each bus\nPd = bus[:, 2]  # Real power demand in MW\nQd = bus[:, 3]  # Reactive power demand in MVAr\n\n# Access generator data\ngen_bus = gen[:, 0].astype(int)  # Bus where generator is connected\nPmax = gen[:, 8]  # Maximum power output\nPmin = gen[:, 9]  # Minimum power output\n\n# Access branch data\nfrom_bus = branch[:, 0].astype(int)\nto_bus = branch[:, 1].astype(int)\nreactance = branch[:, 3]\nrate_a = branch[:, 5]  # Thermal limit in MVA\n```\n\n## Per-Unit Conversion\n\nPower quantities in the data are in Voltage magnitudes are 1.0 p.u. at all buses\n2. Angle differences are small (sin(θ) ≈ θ, cos(θ) ≈ 1)\n3. Line resistances are negligible (R << X)\n4. Reactive power is ignored\n\n## Mathematical Formulation\n\n### Decision Variables\n- P_g: Real power output of generator g (MW)\n- θ_n: Voltage angle at bus n (radians)\n\n### Objective Function\nMinimize total generation cost:\n```\nmin Σ_g C_g(P_g)\n```\n\nFor polynomial cost: C_g(P_g) = a_g * P_g^2 + b_g * P_g + c_g\n\n### Constraints\n\n#### 1. Power Balance at Each Bus\n```\nΣ_{g ∈ G_n} P_g - P_d,n = Σ_{(n,m) ∈ L} P_nm    ∀ bus n\n```\n\nWhere line flow P_nm = (θ_n - θ_m) / X_nm * baseMVA\n\nIn matrix form: B * θ = P_inj (ignoring reference bus)\n\n#### 2. Generator Limits\n```\nP_min,g ≤ P_g ≤ P_max,g    ∀ generator g\n```\n\n#### 3. Transmission Line Thermal Limits\n```\n-Rate_A ≤ P_nm ≤ Rate_A    ∀ line (n,m)\n```\n\nLine flow: P_nm = (θ_n - θ_m) / X_nm * baseMVA\n\n#### 4. Reference Bus\n```\nθ_ref = 0    (slack bus angle fixed)\n```\n\n## Building the Susceptance Matrix B\n\nFor DC power flow, the bus admittance matrix simplifies to the susceptance matrix:\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef build_susceptance_matrix(bus, branch, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Build DC power flow susceptance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    bus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))\n\n    for br in branch:\n        if br[10] == 0:  # BR_STATUS = 0 means out of service\n            continue\n\n        f = int(br[0])  # F_BUS\n        t = int(br[1])  # T_BUS\n        x = br[3]       # BR_X (reactance)\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        b = 1.0 / x  # Susceptance\n\n        fi = bus_to_idx[f]\n        ti = bus_to_idx[t]\n\n        # Update B matrix\n        B[fi, fi] += b\n        B[ti, ti] += b\n        B[fi, ti] -= b\n        B[ti, fi] -= b\n\n    return B, bus_to_idx\n```\n\n## Line Flow Calculation\n\n```python\ndef compute_line_flows(branch, theta, bus_to_idx, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute line flows from bus angles.\"\"\"\n    flows = []\n    for br in branch:\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            flows.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f = int(br[0])\n        t = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6\n\n        fi = bus_to_idx[f]\n        ti = bus_to_idx[t]\n\n        # Flow in MW\n        flow = (theta[fi] - theta[ti]) / x * baseMVA\n        flows.append(flow)\n\n    return np.array(flows)\n```\n\n## LMP Calculation\n\nLocational Marginal Prices (LMPs) are derived from the dual variables:\n\n```\nLMP_n = λ_n  (dual of power balance constraint at bus n)\n```\n\nThe LMP at each bus consists of:\n- Energy component (system marginal price)\n- Congestion component (from binding line constraints)\n- Loss component (zero in DC-OPF)\n\n## CVXPY Implementation Example\n\n```python\nimport cvxpy as cp\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef solve_dcopf(bus, gen, branch, gencost, baseMVA):\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n\n    bus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen, nonneg=True)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)\n\n    # Build incidence and susceptance data\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]  # Load at each bus\n\n    # Build B matrix and constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0 (find slack bus type=3)\n    slack_idx = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_idx) == 0:\n        slack_idx = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_idx[0]] == 0)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    constraints.append(Pg >= Pmin)\n    constraints.append(Pg <= Pmax)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    # Net injection = generation - load\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_to_idx[int(g[0])] for g in gen]\n\n    # Build PTDF-style constraints or nodal balance\n    # ... (see full implementation)\n\n    # Objective: minimize cost\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        # Polynomial cost coefficients\n        if gencost[g, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial\n            ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n            coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n            if ncost == 3:  # Quadratic\n                cost += coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n            elif ncost == 2:  # Linear\n                cost += coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    prob.solve()\n\n    return Pg.value, theta.value, prob.value\n```\n\n## Key Notes\n\n1. For market applications, use RATE_A for normal thermal limits\n2. LMPs are sensitive to which constraints are binding\n3. A binding line creates price separation between buses\n4. Quadratic costs require a QP solver (OSQP, CLARABEL, etc.)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1699,"cache_read_input_tokens":28211,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1699,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fb895624-8769-4efe-ba21-a976446976e0","uuid":"ccb48012-273f-4d4b-8b99-ec96675cb278"}

Reserve Co-optimization in DC-OPF\n\nReserve co-optimization jointly clears energy and reserve markets, ensuring spinning reserves are available while respecting generator capacity limits.\n\n## Spinning Reserve Basics\n\nSpinning reserves are generators synchronized to the grid that can increase output within minutes to respond to contingencies.\n\n## Mathematical Formulation\n\n### Additional Decision Variables\n- r_g: Reserve provided by generator g (MW)\n\n### Objective Function\nMinimize energy cost plus reserve cost:\n```\nmin Σ_g [C_g(P_g) + C_r,g * r_g]\n```\n\nWhere C_r,g is the reserve offer price (often a small premium or zero for mandatory reserves).\n\n### Standard Capacity Coupling Constraint\n\nEach generator's energy output plus reserve cannot exceed its capacity:\n```\nP_g + r_g ≤ P_max,g    ∀ generator g\n```\n\nThis ensures that if the reserve is called, the generator can actually deliver it.\n\n### Reserve Provision Limits\n\nGenerators have limits on how much reserve they can provide:\n```\n0 ≤ r_g ≤ R_max,g    ∀ generator g\n```\n\nWhere R_max,g is the maximum reserve capacity the generator can offer.\n\n### System Reserve Requirement\n\nTotal reserves must meet system requirement:\n```\nΣ_g r_g ≥ R_req\n```\n\nWhere R_req is the minimum spinning reserve requirement.\n\n## Reserve Clearing Price\n\nThe reserve market clearing price (MCP) is the dual variable (shadow price) of the system reserve requirement constraint:\n```\nMCP_reserve = μ    (dual of Σ_g r_g ≥ R_req)\n```\n\n## CVXPY Implementation\n\n```python\nimport cvxpy as cp\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(bus, gen, branch, gencost,\n                               reserve_capacity, reserve_requirement,\n                               baseMVA, reserve_cost_factor=0.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    reserve_capacity : array\n        Maximum reserve each generator can provide (MW)\n    reserve_requirement : float\n        Minimum total system reserve requirement (MW)\n    reserve_cost_factor : float\n        Cost premium for reserves ($/MWh), often 0\n    \"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n\n    bus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)        # Energy dispatch\n    r = cp.Variable(n_gen)         # Reserve allocation\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)     # Bus angles\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # Generator limits\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    constraints.append(Pg >= Pmin)\n    constraints.append(Pg <= Pmax)\n\n    # Reserve limits\n    constraints.append(r >= 0)\n    constraints.append(r <= reserve_capacity)\n\n    # Capacity coupling: P + r <= Pmax\n    constraints.append(Pg + r <= Pmax)\n\n    # Also ensure P + r respects Pmin for energy\n    # r should not cause energy to be negative\n    constraints.append(Pg >= Pmin)\n\n    # System reserve requirement (this constraint's dual is the reserve MCP)\n    reserve_constraint = (cp.sum(r) >= reserve_requirement)\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # ... (add power balance and line constraints)\n\n    # Objective: energy cost + reserve cost\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[g, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial\n            ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n            coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n            if ncost == 3:  # Quadratic\n                cost += coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n            elif ncost == 2:  # Linear\n                cost += coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n\n        # Reserve cost (small premium or zero)\n        cost += reserve_cost_factor * r[g]\n\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL)\n\n    # Extract reserve MCP from dual\n    reserve_mcp = reserve_constraint.dual_value\n\n    return {\n        'Pg': Pg.value,\n        'reserve': r.value,\n        'theta': theta.value,\n        'total_cost': prob.value,\n        'reserve_mcp': float(reserve_mcp) if reserve_mcp else 0.0\n    }\n```\n\n## Interaction with Energy Market\n\nReserve co-optimization can affect LMPs because:\n\n1. **Capacity Competition**: Generators must allocate capacity between energy and reserves\n2. **Price Uplift**: If reserves are valuable, cheap generators may provide reserves instead of energy, raising the marginal energy price\n3. **Opportunity Cost**: Reserve price reflects the opportunity cost of not selling energy\n\n## Example Scenario\n\nConsider a system with:\n- Generator A: 100 MW max, $20/MWh, 20 MW reserve capacity\n- Generator B: 50 MW max, $30/MWh, 10 MW reserve capacity\n- Load: 80 MW\n- Reserve requirement: 25 MW\n\nSolution:\n- Gen A: 80 MW energy + 20 MW reserve (at capacity)\n- Gen B: 0 MW energy + 10 MW reserve (but need 5 MW more)\n\nIf reserve requirement exceeds available capacity, problem is infeasible.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1916,"cache_read_input_tokens":29910,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1916,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fb895624-8769-4efe-ba21-a976446976e0","uuid":"18cb3c58-bc73-4fd2-834e-997552840662"}

(add power balance and line constraints)\n\n    # Objective: energy cost + reserve cost\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[g, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial\n            ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n            coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n            if ncost == 3:  # Quadratic\n                cost += coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n            elif ncost == 2:  # Linear\n                cost += coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n\n        # Reserve cost (small premium or zero)\n        cost += reserve_cost_factor * r[g]\n\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL)\n\n    # Extract reserve MCP from dual\n    reserve_mcp = reserve_constraint.dual_value\n\n    return {\n        'Pg': Pg.value,\n        'reserve': r.value,\n        'theta': theta.value,\n        'total_cost': prob.value,\n        'reserve_mcp': float(reserve_mcp) if reserve_mcp else 0.0\n    }\n```\n\n## Interaction with Energy Market\n\nReserve co-optimization can affect LMPs because:\n\n1. **Capacity Competition**: Generators must allocate capacity between energy and reserves\n2. **Price Uplift**: If reserves are valuable, cheap generators may provide reserves instead of energy, raising the marginal energy price\n3. **Opportunity Cost**: Reserve price reflects the opportunity cost of not selling energy\n\n## Example Scenario\n\nConsider a system with:\n- Generator A: 100 MW max, $20/MWh, 20 MW reserve capacity\n- Generator B: 50 MW max, $30/MWh, 10 MW reserve capacity\n- Load: 80 MW\n- Reserve requirement: 25 MW\n\nSolution:\n- Gen A: 80 MW energy + 20 MW reserve (at capacity)\n- Gen B: 0 MW energy + 10 MW reserve (but need 5 MW more)\n\nIf reserve requirement exceeds available capacity, problem is infeasible.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01FuY8hy9zgYZ45qJYwCVkzQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01GZWopwUPkXhkswVYC4HWgp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/lmp-analysis.md","content":"# Locational Marginal Price (LMP) Analysis\n\nLMPs are the market clearing prices at each bus in a power system, reflecting the marginal cost to serve an additional MW of load at that location.\n\n## LMP Components\n\nIn DC-OPF, LMP at bus n consists of:\n```\nLMP_n = λ_energy + λ_congestion,n\n```\n\nWhere:\n- λ_energy: System energy component (from marginal generator)\n- λ_congestion,n: Location-specific congestion component\n\n## Mathematical Derivation\n\nFrom the Lagrangian of DC-OPF:\n\n```\nL = Σ_g C_g(P_g) - Σ_n λ_n (Σ_{g∈n} P_g - P_d,n - Σ_m B_nm(θ_n - θ_m))\n    - Σ_l μ_l (|flow_l| - Rate_A,l)\n```\n\nThe LMP at bus n equals λ_n, the dual variable of the power balance constraint.\n\n## Congestion and Price Separation\n\nWhen a transmission line is binding (at its thermal limit):\n- Buses on the \"sending\" side have lower LMPs (cheap generation cannot flow through)\n- Buses on the \"receiving\" side have higher LMPs (must use expensive local generation)\n\nPrice separation indicates congestion:\n```\nΔPrice = LMP_receiving - LMP_sending > 0  when line is congested\n```\n\n## Computing LMPs from CVXPY\n\n```python\nimport cvxpy as cp\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef get_lmps_from_dcopf(bus, gen, branch, gencost, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve DC-OPF and return LMPs.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n\n    bus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # Reference bus\n    ref_idx = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_idx) == 0:\n        ref_idx = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_idx[0]] == 0)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    constraints.append(Pg >= Pmin)\n    constraints.append(Pg <= Pmax)\n\n    # Build power balance constraints per bus\n    # and track them for LMP extraction\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Generation injection at each bus\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[g, 0])\n        bus_idx = bus_to_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(g)\n\n    # Branch susceptance matrix (line flows)\n    flows = []\n    for br in branch:\n        if br[10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            flows.append(0)\n            continue\n        f = int(br[0])\n        t = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3] if br[3] != 0 else 1e-6\n        fi = bus_to_idx[f]\n        ti = bus_to_idx[t]\n        flow = (theta[fi] - theta[ti]) / x * baseMVA\n        flows.append(flow)\n\n    # Power balance constraints - stored for dual extraction\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net flow out of bus\n        net_flow = 0\n        for l, br in enumerate(branch):\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f = int(br[0])\n            t = int(br[1])\n            fi = bus_to_idx[f]\n            ti = bus_to_idx[t]\n            if fi == i:\n                net_flow += flows[l]\n            elif ti == i:\n                net_flow -= flows[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # Line flow limits\n    line_constraints = []\n    for l, br in enumerate(branch):\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            continue\n        rate_a = br[5]\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flows[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flows[l] >= -rate_a)\n            line_constraints.append((l, c_upper, c_lower))\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n\n    # Objective\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            cost += coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            cost += coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            cost += coeffs[0]\n\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL)\n\n    # Extract LMPs (negative of dual because of constraint formulation)\n    lmps = []\n    for i, c in enumerate(balance_constraints):\n        lmp = float(c.dual_value) if c.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n        lmps.append({'bus': int(bus_ids[i]), 'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': lmp})\n\n    return lmps, prob.value\n```\n\n## Identifying Binding Lines\n\nA line is binding when its flow equals its thermal limit:\n\n```python\ndef get_binding_lines(branch, flows, threshold=0.99):\n    \"\"\"Identify lines at or near thermal capacity.\"\"\"\n    binding = []\n    for l, br in enumerate(branch):\n        if br[10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n        rate_a = br[5]\n        if rate_a <= 0:\n            continue\n        flow = abs(flows[l])\n        loading = flow / rate_a\n        if loading >= threshold:\n            binding.append({\n                'from': int(br[0]),\n                'to': int(br[1]),\n                'flow_MW': round(flow, 2),\n                'limit_MW': round(rate_a, 2)\n            })\n    return binding\n```\n\n## Congestion Relief Analysis\n\nWhen comparing base case to counterfactual (relaxed constraint):\n\n```python\ndef analyze_congestion_relief(base_lmps, cf_lmps, base_binding, cf_binding, target_line):\n    \"\"\"Analyze impact of relaxing a transmission constraint.\"\"\"\n\n    # Check if target line is still binding\n    target_binding_base = any(\n        (b['from'] == target_line[0] and b['to'] == target_line[1]) or\n        (b['from'] == target_line[1] and b['to'] == target_line[0])\n        for b in base_binding\n    )\n    target_binding_cf = any(\n        (b['from'] == target_line[0] and b['to'] == target_line[1]) or\n        (b['from'] == target_line[1] and b['to'] == target_line[0])\n        for b in cf_binding\n    )\n\n    congestion_relieved = target_binding_base and not target_binding_cf\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    lmp_changes = []\n    base_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in base_lmps}\n    cf_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in cf_lmps}\n\n    for bus in base_lmp_dict:\n        base = base_lmp_dict[bus]\n        cf = cf_lmp_dict.get(bus, base)\n        delta = cf - base\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus,\n            'base_lmp': base,\n            'cf_lmp': cf,\n            'delta': delta\n        })\n\n    # Sort by delta (most negative = largest drop)\n    lmp_changes.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n\n    return {\n        'congestion_relieved': congestion_relieved,\n        'buses_with_largest_lmp_drop': lmp_changes[:3]\n    }\n```\n\n## Interpretation\n\n- **Positive LMP delta**: Price increased (rare when relaxing constraints)\n- **Negative LMP delta**: Price decreased (congestion relief)\n- **Zero delta**: Bus unaffected by the constraint change\n\nLarge LMP drops indicate buses that were previously \"behind\" the congested line.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1928,"cache_read_input_tokens":33398,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1928,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fb895624-8769-4efe-ba21-a976446976e0","uuid":"e5f1a096-0a08-4908-abfe-a8bdb5667a9f"}

Variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # Reference bus\n    ref_idx = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_idx) == 0:\n        ref_idx = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[ref_idx[0]] == 0)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    constraints.append(Pg >= Pmin)\n    constraints.append(Pg <= Pmax)\n\n    # Build power balance constraints per bus\n    # and track them for LMP extraction\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Generation injection at each bus\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[g, 0])\n        bus_idx = bus_to_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(g)\n\n    # Branch susceptance matrix (line flows)\n    flows = []\n    for br in branch:\n        if br[10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            flows.append(0)\n            continue\n        f = int(br[0])\n        t = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3] if br[3] != 0 else 1e-6\n        fi = bus_to_idx[f]\n        ti = bus_to_idx[t]\n        flow = (theta[fi] - theta[ti]) / x * baseMVA\n        flows.append(flow)\n\n    # Power balance constraints - stored for dual extraction\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net flow out of bus\n        net_flow = 0\n        for l, br in enumerate(branch):\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f = int(br[0])\n            t = int(br[1])\n            fi = bus_to_idx[f]\n            ti = bus_to_idx[t]\n            if fi == i:\n                net_flow += flows[l]\n            elif ti == i:\n                net_flow -= flows[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # Line flow limits\n    line_constraints = []\n    for l, br in enumerate(branch):\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            continue\n        rate_a = br[5]\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flows[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flows[l] >= -rate_a)\n            line_constraints.append((l, c_upper, c_lower))\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n\n    # Objective\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            cost += coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            cost += coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            cost += coeffs[0]\n\n    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    prob.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL)\n\n    # Extract LMPs (negative of dual because of constraint formulation)\n    lmps = []\n    for i, c in enumerate(balance_constraints):\n        lmp = float(c.dual_value) if c.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n        lmps.append({'bus': int(bus_ids[i]), 'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': lmp})\n\n    return lmps, prob.value\n```\n\n## Identifying Binding Lines\n\nA line is binding when its flow equals its thermal limit:\n\n```python\ndef get_binding_lines(branch, flows, threshold=0.99):\n    \"\"\"Identify lines at or near thermal capacity.\"\"\"\n    binding = []\n    for l, br in enumerate(branch):\n        if br[10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n        rate_a = br[5]\n        if rate_a <= 0:\n            continue\n        flow = abs(flows[l])\n        loading = flow / rate_a\n        if loading >= threshold:\n            binding.append({\n                'from': int(br[0]),\n                'to': int(br[1]),\n                'flow_MW': round(flow, 2),\n                'limit_MW': round(rate_a, 2)\n            })\n    return binding\n```\n\n## Congestion Relief Analysis\n\nWhen comparing base case to counterfactual (relaxed constraint):\n\n```python\ndef analyze_congestion_relief(base_lmps, cf_lmps, base_binding, cf_binding, target_line):\n    \"\"\"Analyze impact of relaxing a transmission constraint.\"\"\"\n\n    # Check if target line is still binding\n    target_binding_base = any(\n        (b['from'] == target_line[0] and b['to'] == target_line[1]) or\n        (b['from'] == target_line[1] and b['to'] == target_line[0])\n        for b in base_binding\n    )\n    target_binding_cf = any(\n        (b['from'] == target_line[0] and b['to'] == target_line[1]) or\n        (b['from'] == target_line[1] and b['to'] == target_line[0])\n        for b in cf_binding\n    )\n\n    congestion_relieved = target_binding_base and not target_binding_cf\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    lmp_changes = []\n    base_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in base_lmps}\n    cf_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in cf_lmps}\n\n    for bus in base_lmp_dict:\n        base = base_lmp_dict[bus]\n        cf = cf_lmp_dict.get(bus, base)\n        delta = cf - base\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus,\n            'base_lmp': base,\n            'cf_lmp': cf,\n            'delta': delta\n        })\n\n    # Sort by delta (most negative = largest drop)\n    lmp_changes.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n\n    return {\n        'congestion_relieved': congestion_relieved,\n        'buses_with_largest_lmp_drop': lmp_changes[:3]\n    }\n```\n\n## Interpretation\n\n- **Positive LMP delta**: Price increased (rare when relaxing constraints)\n- **Negative LMP delta**: Price decreased (congestion relief)\n- **Zero delta**: Bus unaffected by the constraint change\n\nLarge LMP drops indicate buses that were previously \"behind\" the congested line.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01H9DDrc9eyZTDCHKmgELMvL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01LpWMTNZN4PApGvazVxrFAo","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/market_clearing.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDC-OPF Market Clearing with Reserve Co-optimization\n\nThis script solves a DC optimal power flow problem with spinning reserve\nco-optimization for day-ahead market clearing.\n\nModel:\n- Objective: Minimize total generation cost\n- Constraints:\n  1. Power balance at each bus (DC approximation)\n  2. Generator thermal limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n  3. Transmission line thermal limits (|flow| <= Rate_A)\n  4. Spinning reserve requirements with capacity coupling (Pg + r <= Pmax)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nfrom scipy import sparse\n\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': data['baseMVA'],\n        'bus': np.array(data['bus']),\n        'gen': np.array(data['gen']),\n        'branch': np.array(data['branch']),\n        'gencost': np.array(data['gencost']),\n        'reserve_capacity': np.array(data['reserve_capacity']),\n        'reserve_requirement': data['reserve_requirement']\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    network : dict\n        Network data from load_network()\n    line_capacity_override : dict, optional\n        Override capacity for specific lines: {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity}\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict with results including LMPs, costs, binding lines, reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    branch = network['branch'].copy()  # Copy to allow modifications\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    reserve_capacity = network['reserve_capacity']\n    reserve_requirement = network['reserve_requirement']\n\n    # Apply line capacity overrides\n    if line_capacity_override:\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch):\n            f_bus, t_bus = int(br[0]), int(br[1])\n            key = (f_bus, t_bus)\n            key_rev = (t_bus, f_bus)\n            if key in line_capacity_override:\n                branch[i, 5] = line_capacity_override[key]  # RATE_A\n            elif key_rev in line_capacity_override:\n                branch[i, 5] = line_capacity_override[key_rev]\n\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n\n    bus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)        # Generator power output (MW)\n    r = cp.Variable(n_gen)         # Reserve allocation (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)     # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # ===== Reference bus (slack bus, type=3) =====\n    slack_indices = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_indices) == 0:\n        slack_indices = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_indices[0]] == 0)\n\n    # ===== Generator limits =====\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7]  # 1=in-service, 0=out-of-service\n\n    # Only active generators can produce\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(r[g] == 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= Pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # ===== Reserve constraints =====\n    # Reserve must be non-negative and within capacity\n    constraints.append(r >= 0)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(r[g] <= reserve_capacity[g])\n            # Capacity coupling: Pg + r <= Pmax\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + r[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # System reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = (cp.sum(r) >= reserve_requirement)\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # ===== Build line flow expressions =====\n    # Line flow: flow_l = (theta_from - theta_to) / X * baseMVA\n    flow_exprs = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:  # BR_STATUS = 0, out of service\n            flow_exprs.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        flow_exprs.append(flow)\n\n    # ===== Line thermal limits =====\n    line_constraints_upper = []\n    line_constraints_lower = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = br[5]  # RATE_A thermal limit\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flow_exprs[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flow_exprs[l] >= -rate_a)\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n            line_constraints_upper.append(c_upper)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(c_lower)\n        else:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n\n    # ===== Power balance at each bus =====\n    # Map generators to buses\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus = int(gen[g, 0])\n        if gen_bus in bus_to_idx:\n            gen_at_bus[bus_to_idx[gen_bus]].append(g)\n\n    # Load at each bus\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Total generation at bus\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i])\n        else:\n            gen_sum = 0\n\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net power flow out of bus\n        net_flow_out = 0\n        for l in range(n_branch):\n            br = branch[l]\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f_bus = int(br[0])\n            t_bus = int(br[1])\n            f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n            if f_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out + flow_exprs[l]\n            elif t_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out - flow_exprs[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow_out)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # ===== Objective function: Minimize generation cost =====\n    # Polynomial cost: C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        model = int(gencost[g, 0])\n        if model != 2:\n            continue  # Only polynomial cost supported\n\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: a*P^2 + b*P + c\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            # Linear: a*P + b\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            # Constant\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0]\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # ===== Extract results =====\n\n    # Total cost\n    total_cost = problem.value\n\n    # LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        dual_val = balance_constraints[i].dual_value\n        lmp = float(dual_val) if dual_val is not None else 0.0\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': int(bus_ids[i]),\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmp, 6)\n        })\n\n    # Reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = float(reserve_constraint.dual_value) if reserve_constraint.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n\n    # Compute actual line flows for binding analysis\n    theta_val = theta.value\n    actual_flows = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            actual_flows.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3] if br[3] != 0 else 1e-6\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta_val[f_idx] - theta_val[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        actual_flows.append(flow)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus (DC approximation)\n  2. Generator thermal limits (Pmin <= Pg <= Pmax)\n  3. Transmission line thermal limits (|flow| <= Rate_A)\n  4. Spinning reserve requirements with capacity coupling (Pg + r <= Pmax)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nfrom scipy import sparse\n\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': data['baseMVA'],\n        'bus': np.array(data['bus']),\n        'gen': np.array(data['gen']),\n        'branch': np.array(data['branch']),\n        'gencost': np.array(data['gencost']),\n        'reserve_capacity': np.array(data['reserve_capacity']),\n        'reserve_requirement': data['reserve_requirement']\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    network : dict\n        Network data from load_network()\n    line_capacity_override : dict, optional\n        Override capacity for specific lines: {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity}\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict with results including LMPs, costs, binding lines, reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    branch = network['branch'].copy()  # Copy to allow modifications\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    reserve_capacity = network['reserve_capacity']\n    reserve_requirement = network['reserve_requirement']\n\n    # Apply line capacity overrides\n    if line_capacity_override:\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch):\n            f_bus, t_bus = int(br[0]), int(br[1])\n            key = (f_bus, t_bus)\n            key_rev = (t_bus, f_bus)\n            if key in line_capacity_override:\n                branch[i, 5] = line_capacity_override[key]  # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)        # Generator power output (MW)\n    r = cp.Variable(n_gen)         # Reserve allocation (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)     # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # ===== Reference bus (slack bus, type=3) =====\n    slack_indices = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_indices) == 0:\n        slack_indices = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_indices[0]] == 0)\n\n    # ===== Generator limits =====\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7]  # 1=in-service, 0=out-of-service\n\n    # Only active generators can produce\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(r[g] == 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= Pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # ===== Reserve constraints =====\n    # Reserve must be non-negative and within capacity\n    constraints.append(r >= 0)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(r[g] <= reserve_capacity[g])\n            # Capacity coupling: Pg + r <= Pmax\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + r[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # System reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = (cp.sum(r) >= reserve_requirement)\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # ===== Build line flow expressions =====\n    # Line flow: flow_l = (theta_from - theta_to) / X * baseMVA\n    flow_exprs = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:  # BR_STATUS = 0, out of service\n            flow_exprs.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        flow_exprs.append(flow)\n\n    # ===== Line thermal limits =====\n    line_constraints_upper = []\n    line_constraints_lower = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = br[5]  # RATE_A thermal limit\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flow_exprs[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flow_exprs[l] >= -rate_a)\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n            line_constraints_upper.append(c_upper)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(c_lower)\n        else:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n\n    # ===== Power balance at each bus =====\n    # Map generators to buses\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus = int(gen[g, 0])\n        if gen_bus in bus_to_idx:\n            gen_at_bus[bus_to_idx[gen_bus]].append(g)\n\n    # Load at each bus\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Total generation at bus\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i])\n        else:\n            gen_sum = 0\n\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net power flow out of bus\n        net_flow_out = 0\n        for l in range(n_branch):\n            br = branch[l]\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f_bus = int(br[0])\n            t_bus = int(br[1])\n            f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n            if f_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out + flow_exprs[l]\n            elif t_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out - flow_exprs[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow_out)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # ===== Objective function: Minimize generation cost =====\n    # Polynomial cost: C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        model = int(gencost[g, 0])\n        if model != 2:\n            continue  # Only polynomial cost supported\n\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: a*P^2 + b*P + c\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            # Linear: a*P + b\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            # Constant\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0]\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = float(reserve_constraint.dual_value) if reserve_constraint.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n\n    # Compute actual line flows for binding analysis\n    theta_val = theta.value\n    actual_flows = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            actual_flows.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3] if br[3] != 0 else 1e-6\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta_val[f_idx] - theta_val[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        actual_flows.append(flow)\n\n    # Find binding lines (loading >= 99%)\n    binding_lines = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            continue\n        rate_a = br[5]\n        if rate_a <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        flow = abs(actual_flows[l])\n        loading = flow / rate_a\n\n        if loading >= 0.99:\n            binding_lines.append({\n                'from': int(br[0]),\n                'to': int(br[1]),\n                'flow_MW': round(flow, 2),\n                'limit_MW': round(rate_a, 2)\n            })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': round(total_cost, 2),\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': round(reserve_mcp, 6),\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg.value,\n        'reserve': r.value,\n        'theta': theta_val,\n        'flows': actual_flows\n    }\n\n\ndef run_market_analysis(network_file, target_line, capacity_increase_pct=0.20):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run market clearing for base case and counterfactual scenario.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    network_file : str\n        Path to network JSON file\n    target_line : tuple\n        (from_bus, to_bus) of the line to modify\n    capacity_increase_pct : float\n        Percentage to increase capacity (0.20 = 20%)\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict with base_case, counterfactual, and impact_analysis\n    \"\"\"\n    # Spinning reserve requirements with capacity coupling (Pg + r <= Pmax)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nfrom scipy import sparse\n\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': data['baseMVA'],\n        'bus': np.array(data['bus']),\n        'gen': np.array(data['gen']),\n        'branch': np.array(data['branch']),\n        'gencost': np.array(data['gencost']),\n        'reserve_capacity': np.array(data['reserve_capacity']),\n        'reserve_requirement': data['reserve_requirement']\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    network : dict\n        Network data from load_network()\n    line_capacity_override : dict, optional\n        Override capacity for specific lines: {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity}\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict with results including LMPs, costs, binding lines, reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    branch = network['branch'].copy()  # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)        # Generator power output (MW)\n    r = cp.Variable(n_gen)         # Reserve allocation (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)     # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # ===== Reference bus (slack bus, type=3) =====\n    slack_indices = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_indices) == 0:\n        slack_indices = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_indices[0]] == 0)\n\n    # ===== Generator limits =====\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7]  # 1=in-service, 0=out-of-service\n\n    # Only active generators can produce\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(r[g] == 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= Pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # ===== Reserve constraints =====\n    # Reserve must be non-negative and within capacity\n    constraints.append(r >= 0)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(r[g] <= reserve_capacity[g])\n            # ===== Build line flow expressions =====\n    # Line flow: flow_l = (theta_from - theta_to) / X * baseMVA\n    flow_exprs = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:  # BR_STATUS = 0, out of service\n            flow_exprs.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        flow_exprs.append(flow)\n\n    # ===== Line thermal limits =====\n    line_constraints_upper = []\n    line_constraints_lower = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = br[5]  # RATE_A thermal limit\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flow_exprs[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flow_exprs[l] >= -rate_a)\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n            line_constraints_upper.append(c_upper)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(c_lower)\n        else:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n\n    # ===== Power balance at each bus =====\n    # Map generators to buses\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus = int(gen[g, 0])\n        if gen_bus in bus_to_idx:\n            gen_at_bus[bus_to_idx[gen_bus]].append(g)\n\n    # Load at each bus\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Total generation at bus\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i])\n        else:\n            gen_sum = 0\n\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net power flow out of bus\n        net_flow_out = 0\n        for l in range(n_branch):\n            br = branch[l]\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f_bus = int(br[0])\n            t_bus = int(br[1])\n            f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n            if f_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out + flow_exprs[l]\n            elif t_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out - flow_exprs[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow_out)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # ===== Objective function: Minimize generation cost =====\n    # Polynomial cost: C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        model = int(gencost[g, 0])\n        if model != 2:\n            continue  # Only polynomial cost supported\n\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: a*P^2 + b*P + c\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            # Linear: a*P + b\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            # Constant\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0]\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = float(reserve_constraint.dual_value) if reserve_constraint.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n\n    # Compute actual line flows for binding analysis\n    theta_val = theta.value\n    actual_flows = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            actual_flows.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3] if br[3] != 0 else 1e-6\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta_val[f_idx] - theta_val[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        actual_flows.append(flow)\n\n    # Load network\n    network = load_network(network_file)\n\n    # Find original capacity of target line\n    branch = network['branch']\n    original_capacity = None\n    for br in branch:\n        f_bus, t_bus = int(br[0]), int(br[1])\n        if (f_bus == target_line[0] and t_bus == target_line[1]) or \\\n           (f_bus == target_line[1] and t_bus == target_line[0]):\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            break\n\n    if original_capacity is None:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Line {target_line} not found in network\")\n\n    print(f\"Target line {target_line}: original capacity = {original_capacity} MW\")\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * (1 + capacity_increase_pct)\n    print(f\"Counterfactual capacity = {new_capacity} MW (+{capacity_increase_pct*100}%)\")\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\nSolving base case...\")\n    base_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network)\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${base_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual\n    print(\"\\nSolving counterfactual...\")\n    cf_override = {target_line: new_capacity}\n    cf_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=cf_override)\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour'] - cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    base_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus_id in base_lmp_dict:\n        base_lmp = base_lmp_dict[bus_id]\n        cf_lmp = cf_lmp_dict.get(bus_id, base_lmp)\n        delta = cf_lmp - base_lmp\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'base_lmp': round(base_lmp, 6),\n            'cf_lmp': round(cf_lmp, 6),\n            'delta': round(delta, 6)\n        })\n\n    # ===== Build line flow expressions =====\n    # Line flow: flow_l = (theta_from - theta_to) / X * baseMVA\n    flow_exprs = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:  # BR_STATUS = 0, out of service\n            flow_exprs.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        flow_exprs.append(flow)\n\n    # ===== Line thermal limits =====\n    line_constraints_upper = []\n    line_constraints_lower = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = br[5]  # RATE_A thermal limit\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flow_exprs[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flow_exprs[l] >= -rate_a)\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n            line_constraints_upper.append(c_upper)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(c_lower)\n        else:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n\n    # ===== Power balance at each bus =====\n    # Map generators to buses\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus = int(gen[g, 0])\n        if gen_bus in bus_to_idx:\n            gen_at_bus[bus_to_idx[gen_bus]].append(g)\n\n    # Load at each bus\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Total generation at bus\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i])\n        else:\n            gen_sum = 0\n\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net power flow out of bus\n        net_flow_out = 0\n        for l in range(n_branch):\n            br = branch[l]\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f_bus = int(br[0])\n            t_bus = int(br[1])\n            f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n            if f_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out + flow_exprs[l]\n            elif t_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out - flow_exprs[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow_out)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # ===== Objective function: Minimize generation cost =====\n    # Polynomial cost: C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        model = int(gencost[g, 0])\n        if model != 2:\n            continue  # Only polynomial cost supported\n\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: a*P^2 + b*P + c\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            # Linear: a*P + b\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            # Constant\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0]\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # ===== Extract results =====\n\n    # Total cost\n    total_cost = problem.value\n\n    # LMPs from power balance constraint duals\n    lmp_by_bus = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        dual_val = balance_constraints[i].dual_value\n        lmp = float(dual_val) if dual_val is not None else 0.0\n        lmp_by_bus.append({\n            'bus': int(bus_ids[i]),\n            'lmp_dollars_per_MWh': round(lmp, 1)\n        })\n\n    # Reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = float(reserve_constraint.dual_value) if reserve_constraint.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n\n    # Compute actual line flows for binding analysis\n    theta_val = theta.value\n    actual_flows = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            actual_flows.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3] if br[3] != 0 else 1e-6\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta_val[f_idx] - theta_val[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        actual_flows.append(flow)\n\n    # Find binding lines (loading >= 99%)\n    binding_lines = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            continue\n        rate_a = br[5]\n        if rate_a <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        flow = abs(actual_flows[l])\n        loading = flow / rate_a\n\n        if loading >= 0.99:\n            binding_lines.append({\n                'from': int(br[0]),\n                'to': int(br[1]),\n                'flow_MW': round(flow, 2),\n                'limit_MW': round(rate_a, 2)\n            })\n\n    return {\n        'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': round(total_cost, 2),\n        'lmp_by_bus': lmp_by_bus,\n        'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': round(reserve_mcp, 6),\n        'binding_lines': binding_lines,\n        'Pg': Pg.value,\n        'reserve': r.value,\n        'theta': theta_val,\n        'flows': actual_flows\n    }\n\n\ndef run_market_analysis(network_file, target_line, capacity_increase_pct=0.20):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run market clearing for base case and counterfactual scenario.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    network_file : str\n        Path to network JSON file\n    target_line : tuple\n        (from_bus, to_bus) of the line to modify\n    capacity_increase_pct : float\n        Percentage to increase capacity (0.20 = 20%)\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict with base_case, counterfactual, and impact_analysis\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load network\n    network = load_network(network_file)\n\n    # Find original capacity of target line\n    branch = network['branch']\n    original_capacity = None\n    for br in branch:\n        f_bus, t_bus = int(br[0]), int(br[1])\n        if (f_bus == target_line[0] and t_bus == target_line[1]) or \\\n           (f_bus == target_line[1] and t_bus == target_line[0]):\n            original_capacity = br[5]\n            break\n\n    if original_capacity is None:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Line {target_line} not found in network\")\n\n    print(f\"Target line {target_line}: original capacity = {original_capacity} MW\")\n    new_capacity = original_capacity * (1 + capacity_increase_pct)\n    print(f\"Counterfactual capacity = {new_capacity} MW (+{capacity_increase_pct*100}%)\")\n\n    # Run base case\n    print(\"\\nSolving base case...\")\n    base_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network)\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${base_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Binding lines: {len(base_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Run counterfactual\n    print(\"\\nSolving counterfactual...\")\n    cf_override = {target_line: new_capacity}\n    cf_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=cf_override)\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour'] - cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    base_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus_id in base_lmp_dict:\n        base_lmp = base_lmp_dict[bus_id]\n        cf_lmp = cf_lmp_dict.get(bus_id, base_lmp)\n        delta = cf_lmp - base_lmp\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'base_lmp': round(base_lmp, 6),\n            'cf_lmp': round(cf_lmp, 6),\n            'delta': round(delta, 6)\n        })\n\n    # Sort by delta (most negative first = largest drop)\n    lmp_changes.sort(key=lambda x: x['delta'])\n    top_3_drops = lmp_changes[:3]\n\n    # Check if target line is still binding in counterfactual\n    target_binding_in_cf = any(\n        (bl['from'] == target_line[0] and bl['to'] == target_line[1]) or\n        (bl['from'] == target_line[1] and bl['to'] == target_line[0])\n        for bl in cf_result['binding_lines']\n    )\n    congestion_relieved = not target_binding_in_cf\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        'base_case': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': base_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour'],\n            'lmp_by_bus': base_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': base_result['reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh'],\n            'binding_lines': base_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        'counterfactual': {\n            'total_cost_dollars_per_hour': cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour'],\n            'lmp_by_bus': cf_result['lmp_by_bus'],\n            'reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh': cf_result['reserve_mcp_dollars_per_MWh'],\n            'binding_lines': cf_result['binding_lines']\n        },\n        'impact_analysis': {\n            'cost_reduction_dollars_per_hour': round(cost_reduction, 2),\n            'buses_with_largest_lmp_drop': top_3_drops,\n            'congestion_relieved': congestion_relieved\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    # Spinning reserve requirements with capacity coupling (Pg + r <= Pmax)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nfrom scipy import sparse\n\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER-format network from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': data['baseMVA'],\n        'bus': np.array(data['bus']),\n        'gen': np.array(data['gen']),\n        'branch': np.array(data['branch']),\n        'gencost': np.array(data['gencost']),\n        'reserve_capacity': np.array(data['reserve_capacity']),\n        'reserve_requirement': data['reserve_requirement']\n    }\n\n\ndef solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF with reserve co-optimization.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    network : dict\n        Network data from load_network()\n    line_capacity_override : dict, optional\n        Override capacity for specific lines: {(from_bus, to_bus): new_capacity}\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict with results including LMPs, costs, binding lines, reserve MCP\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    branch = network['branch'].copy()  # Copy to allow modifications\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    reserve_capacity = network['reserve_capacity']\n    reserve_requirement = network['reserve_requirement']\n\n    # Apply line capacity overrides\n    if line_capacity_override:\n        for i, br in enumerate(branch):\n            f_bus, t_bus = int(br[0]), int(br[1])\n            key = (f_bus, t_bus)\n            key_rev = (t_bus, f_bus)\n            if key in line_capacity_override:\n                branch[i, 5] = line_capacity_override[key]  # RATE_A\n            elif key_rev in line_capacity_override:\n                branch[i, 5] = line_capacity_override[key_rev]\n\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n\n    bus_ids = bus[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_to_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    Pg = cp.Variable(n_gen)        # Generator power output (MW)\n    r = cp.Variable(n_gen)         # Reserve allocation (MW)\n    theta = cp.Variable(n_bus)     # Bus voltage angles (radians)\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # ===== Reference bus (slack bus, type=3) =====\n    slack_indices = np.where(bus[:, 1] == 3)[0]\n    if len(slack_indices) == 0:\n        slack_indices = [0]\n    constraints.append(theta[slack_indices[0]] == 0)\n\n    # ===== Generator limits =====\n    Pmax = gen[:, 8]\n    Pmin = gen[:, 9]\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7]  # 1=in-service, 0=out-of-service\n\n    # Only active generators can produce\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] == 0)\n            constraints.append(r[g] == 0)\n        else:\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] >= Pmin[g])\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # ===== Reserve constraints =====\n    # Reserve must be non-negative and within capacity\n    constraints.append(r >= 0)\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 1:\n            constraints.append(r[g] <= reserve_capacity[g])\n            # Capacity coupling: Pg + r <= Pmax\n            constraints.append(Pg[g] + r[g] <= Pmax[g])\n\n    # System reserve requirement\n    reserve_constraint = (cp.sum(r) >= reserve_requirement)\n    constraints.append(reserve_constraint)\n\n    # ===== Build line flow expressions =====\n    # Line flow: flow_l = (theta_from - theta_to) / X * baseMVA\n    flow_exprs = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:  # BR_STATUS = 0, out of service\n            flow_exprs.append(0)\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(br[0])\n        t_bus = int(br[1])\n        x = br[3]  # Reactance\n\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 1e-6  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n        t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n        flow = (theta[f_idx] - theta[t_idx]) / x * baseMVA\n        flow_exprs.append(flow)\n\n    # ===== Line thermal limits =====\n    line_constraints_upper = []\n    line_constraints_lower = []\n    for l in range(n_branch):\n        br = branch[l]\n        if br[10] == 0:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n            continue\n\n        rate_a = br[5]  # RATE_A thermal limit\n        if rate_a > 0:\n            c_upper = (flow_exprs[l] <= rate_a)\n            c_lower = (flow_exprs[l] >= -rate_a)\n            constraints.append(c_upper)\n            constraints.append(c_lower)\n            line_constraints_upper.append(c_upper)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(c_lower)\n        else:\n            line_constraints_upper.append(None)\n            line_constraints_lower.append(None)\n\n    # ===== Power balance at each bus =====\n    # Map generators to buses\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        gen_bus = int(gen[g, 0])\n        if gen_bus in bus_to_idx:\n            gen_at_bus[bus_to_idx[gen_bus]].append(g)\n\n    # Load at each bus\n    Pd = bus[:, 2]\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    balance_constraints = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Total generation at bus\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            gen_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in gen_at_bus[i])\n        else:\n            gen_sum = 0\n\n        load = Pd[i]\n\n        # Net power flow out of bus\n        net_flow_out = 0\n        for l in range(n_branch):\n            br = branch[l]\n            if br[10] == 0:\n                continue\n            f_bus = int(br[0])\n            t_bus = int(br[1])\n            f_idx = bus_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = bus_to_idx[t_bus]\n\n            if f_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out + flow_exprs[l]\n            elif t_idx == i:\n                net_flow_out = net_flow_out - flow_exprs[l]\n\n        # Power balance: Gen - Load = Net flow out\n        c = (gen_sum - load == net_flow_out)\n        balance_constraints.append(c)\n        constraints.append(c)\n\n    # ===== Objective function: Minimize generation cost =====\n    # Polynomial cost: C(P) = a*P^2 + b*P + c\n    cost = 0\n    for g in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[g] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        model = int(gencost[g, 0])\n        if model != 2:\n            continue  # Only polynomial cost supported\n\n        ncost = int(gencost[g, 3])\n        coeffs = gencost[g, 4:4+ncost]\n\n        if ncost >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: a*P^2 + b*P + c\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * cp.square(Pg[g]) + coeffs[1] * Pg[g] + coeffs[2]\n        elif ncost == 2:\n            # Linear: a*P + b\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0] * Pg[g] + coeffs[1]\n        elif ncost == 1:\n            # Constant\n            cost = cost + coeffs[0]\n\n    # ===== Solve =====\n    problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cost), constraints)\n    problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=False)\n\n    if problem.status not in ['optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate']:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Optimization failed with status: {problem.status}\")\n\n    # Reserve MCP from reserve requirement constraint dual\n    reserve_mcp = float(reserve_constraint.dual_value) if reserve_constraint.dual_value is not None else 0.0\n    reserve_mcp = round(reserve_mcp, 1)\n\n    # Run counterfactual\n    print(\"\\nSolving counterfactual...\")\n    cf_override = {target_line: new_capacity}\n    cf_result = solve_dcopf_with_reserves(network, line_capacity_override=cf_override)\n    print(f\"  Total cost: ${cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"  Binding lines: {len(cf_result['binding_lines'])}\")\n\n    # Impact analysis\n    cost_reduction = base_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour'] - cf_result['total_cost_dollars_per_hour']\n\n    # Find buses with largest LMP drops\n    base_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in base_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n    cf_lmp_dict = {l['bus']: l['lmp_dollars_per_MWh'] for l in cf_result['lmp_by_bus']}\n\n    lmp_changes = []\n    for bus_id in base_lmp_dict:\n        base_lmp = base_lmp_dict[bus_id]\n        cf_lmp = cf_lmp_dict.get(bus_id, base_lmp)\n        delta = cf_lmp - base_lmp\n        lmp_changes.append({\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'base_lmp': round(base_lmp, 6),\n            'cf_lmp': round(cf_lmp, 6),\n            'delta': round(delta, 6)\n        })\n\n    #
